BÁO CÁO & ĐẶC TẢ CHỈNH SỬA GIAO DIỆN B2B (CHUẨN BẢO MẬT PRODUCTION) — TPS1 ORDER WEBAPP

Tài liệu chuyên biệt dành cho AI Engineer đọc hiểu và thực thi ngay. Chỉ chứa đúng các đoạn code đã chỉnh sửa (Before → After) và các component UI mới, đã loại bỏ 100% các phần code chưa chỉnh sửa và loại bỏ hoàn toàn mọi chỉnh sửa liên quan đến bypass đăng nhập / demo mock.

✓ 35 File Giao Diện & Đa Ngôn Ngữ ✓ 191 Đoạn Chỉnh Sửa Chính Xác (Chỉ Diff/Patch) ✓ 100% An Toàn Bảo Mật Production (Không Đụng Auth/API) ✓ Đa Ngôn Ngữ: Tiếng Việt · English · 中文
CAM KẾT BẢO MẬT PRODUCTION: Báo cáo này đã loại bỏ hoàn toàn mọi thay đổi trong AuthContext.tsx, lib/api.ts, App.tsx và không bao gồm lib/demoMock.ts. Quy trình đăng nhập, token xác thực và kết nối API thật của hệ thống Production được giữ nguyên vẹn 100%.

PROMPT TỔNG HỢP CHO AI THỰC THI (AI-TO-AI INSTRUCTIONS)

Copy đoạn hướng dẫn này kèm các đoạn sửa bên dưới để AI khác áp dụng ngay lập tức.

Bạn là Senior Frontend Engineer. Hãy áp dụng toàn bộ các chỉnh sửa nâng cấp UI/UX B2B, Mobile Responsive và Đa ngôn ngữ (Việt / Anh / Trung) trong tài liệu này vào thư mục order-webapp của dự án Production.

QUY TẮC BẢO MẬT TUYỆT ĐỐI (KHÔNG ĐƯỢC VI PHẠM):
1. TUYỆT ĐỐI KHÔNG chỉnh sửa các file xác thực và kết nối API gốc:
   - KHÔNG sửa src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx
   - KHÔNG sửa src/lib/api.ts
   - KHÔNG sửa src/App.tsx
   - KHÔNG tạo file src/lib/demoMock.ts và KHÔNG thêm bất kỳ cơ chế bypass đăng nhập nào.
2. Chỉ áp dụng đúng 35 file giao diện & đa ngôn ngữ được liệt kê trong báo cáo này:
   - Tạo mới 4 file UI thuần túy: src/hooks/useHorizontalScroll.ts, src/components/products/LineNoteEditor.tsx, src/components/store/SmartSearchBox.tsx, src/components/cart/ExcelOrderSection.tsx.
   - Với các file còn lại: Đối chiếu đúng đoạn "TÌM TRONG FILE (BEFORE)" và thay thế bằng đoạn "THAY BẰNG (AFTER)" tương ứng.
3. Sau khi áp dụng xong, chạy lệnh "npm run build" trong thư mục order-webapp để kiểm tra TypeScript và Vite build thành công 100% (0 lỗi).

Đoạn sửa #9 · Dòng gốc ~602 → Dòng mới ~1021 export function useLanguage() {
1. TÌM ĐOẠN CODE GỐC (BEFORE — Dòng ~602)
}

export function LanguageSwitcher({ dark = false }: { dark?: boolean }) {
  const { language, setLanguage } = useLanguage();
  return <label className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs ${dark ? 'text-white' : 'text-[#17231d]'}`}>
    <span className="sr-only">Language</span>
    <select aria-label="Language / Ngôn ngữ / 语言" value={language} onChange={event => setLanguage(event.target.value as Language)}
      className={`min-h-11 rounded-xl border px-2 font-semibold ${dark ? 'border-white/30 bg-[#0b4f34] text-white' : 'border-[#17231d]/15 bg-white text-[#17231d]'}`}>
      <option value="vi">Tiếng Việt</option><option value="en">English</option><option value="zh-CN">简体中文</option>
    </select>
  </label>;
}
2. THAY BẰNG ĐOẠN ĐÃ SỬA (AFTER — Dòng ~1021)
}

const LANGUAGE_OPTIONS: { code: Language; short: string; label: string }[] = [
  { code: 'vi', short: 'VI', label: 'Tiếng Việt' },
  { code: 'en', short: 'EN', label: 'English' },
  { code: 'zh-CN', short: '中文', label: '简体中文' },
];

export function LanguageSwitcher({ dark = false }: { dark?: boolean }) {
  const { language, setLanguage, t } = useLanguage();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const handlePointerDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (rootRef.current && !rootRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handlePointerDown);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handlePointerDown);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [open]);

  const current = LANGUAGE_OPTIONS.find((opt) => opt.code === language) ?? LANGUAGE_OPTIONS[0];

  return (
    <div ref={rootRef} className="relative">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((prev) => !prev)}
        aria-label={t('Chọn ngôn ngữ')}
        aria-haspopup="listbox"
        aria-expanded={open}
        className={`flex h-9 sm:h-10 items-center gap-1 sm:gap-1.5 rounded-xl border px-2 sm:px-3 text-xs sm:text-sm transition-all cursor-pointer ${
          dark
            ? 'border-white/30 bg-[#0b4f34] text-white font-bold hover:bg-[#0f6443]'
            : open
              ? 'border-[#0f7a4f] bg-white text-[#17231d] font-extrabold sm:font-bold ring-2 ring-[#0f7a4f]/15'
              : 'border-[#17231d]/12 bg-[#f8faf7] text-[#17231d] font-extrabold sm:font-bold hover:bg-white hover:border-[#0f7a4f]/40'
        }`}
      >
        <Globe size={14} className={`hidden sm:inline shrink-0 ${dark ? 'text-white/80' : 'text-[#0f7a4f]'}`} />
        <span className="sm:hidden">{current.short}</span>
        <span className="hidden sm:inline whitespace-nowrap">{current.label}</span>
        <ChevronDown
          size={14}
          className={`shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 ${open ? 'rotate-180' : ''} ${
            dark ? 'text-white/80' : 'text-[#59665f]'
          }`}
        />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          role="listbox"
          className="absolute right-0 top-[calc(100%+6px)] z-50 min-w-41 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white p-1.5 shadow-xl"
        >
          {LANGUAGE_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
            const active = opt.code === language;
            return (
              <button
                key={opt.code}
                type="button"
                role="option"
                aria-selected={active}
                onClick={() => {
                  setLanguage(opt.code);
                  setOpen(false);
                }}
                className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2.5 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-2 text-left text-xs sm:text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-[#eaf6ef] font-bold text-[#0b4f34]'
                    : 'font-medium text-[#17231d] hover:bg-[#f4f8f5]'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap">
                  <span className="inline-flex min-w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-[#17231d]/6 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-extrabold text-[#59665f]">
                    {opt.short}
                  </span>
                  <span>{opt.label}</span>
                </span>
                {active && <Check size={14} className="text-[#0f7a4f] shrink-0" />}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function SearchBox({ className = '' }: { className?: string }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const [value, setValue] = useState(location.pathname === '/tim-kiem' ? params.get('q') || '' : '');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (location.pathname !== '/tim-kiem') setValue('');
  }, [location.pathname]);

  return (
    <form
      role="search"
      className={`relative ${className}`}
      onSubmit={(e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const q = value.trim();
        if (q) navigate(`/tim-kiem?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);
      }}
    >
      <Search size={18} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#8a9690]" />
      <input
        type="search"
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
        placeholder={t('Tìm rau củ, thịt cá, gia vị...')}
        aria-label={t('Tìm sản phẩm')}
        enterKeyHint="search"
        className="h-11 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-[#f5f7f3] pl-10 pr-24 text-sm text-[#17231d] placeholder:text-[#8a9690] focus:border-[#0f7a4f] focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#0f7a4f]/15"
      />
      <button type="submit" className="absolute right-1.5 top-1/2 h-8 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] px-4 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34]">
        {t('Tìm')}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

          <div role="status" className="mb-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-amber-900 ring-1 ring-amber-200">

Tạo file mới: order-webapp/src/components/store/SmartSearchBox.tsx 
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  Search,
  X,
  Zap,
  FileSpreadsheet,
  Flame,
  LayoutGrid,
  Clock,
  Trash2,
  Plus,
  ChevronRight,
  PackageOpen,
  Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { api, type Product } from '../../lib/api';
import { useCart } from '../../contexts/CartContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../../contexts/LanguageContext';
import { useStoreHome } from '../../hooks/useStoreHome';
import { searchProducts } from '../../lib/productSearch';
import ProductThumbnail from '../products/ProductThumbnail';
import { scrollToExcelOrderSection } from '../cart/ExcelOrderSection';

const RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY = 'tps1_recent_searches_v1';
const MAX_RECENT_SEARCHES = 8;

const TRENDING_KEYWORDS = [
  'Ba rọi heo',
  'Cá nục',
  'Trứng gà',
  'Cà chua',
  'Chanh',
  'Hành tây',
  'Cà rốt',
  'Dưa leo',
  'Hành lá',
  'Xà lách',
  'Dầu ăn',
  'Đậu hũ',
];

function getStoredRecentSearches(): string[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY);
    if (!raw) return [];
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter((s): s is string => typeof s === 'string' && Boolean(s.trim())) : [];
  } catch {
    return [];
  }
}

function money(v: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN').format(Number(v) || 0) + 'đ';
}

interface SmartSearchBoxProps {
  className?: string;
}

export default function SmartSearchBox({ className = '' }: SmartSearchBoxProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const { t, productName, productUnit } = useLanguage();
  const { addItem, quantityOf } = useCart();
  const { data: home } = useStoreHome();

  const isQuickOrderPage = location.pathname === '/dat-hang';
  const isSearchPage = location.pathname === '/tim-kiem';

  const [value, setValue] = useState(
    isSearchPage || isQuickOrderPage ? params.get('q') || '' : ''
  );
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [mobilePopoverTop, setMobilePopoverTop] = useState<number>(144);
  const [isMobileViewport, setIsMobileViewport] = useState<boolean>(() =>
    typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerWidth < 640 : true
  );
  const [recentSearches, setRecentSearches] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [liveProducts, setLiveProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [isSearching, setIsSearching] = useState(false);

  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const updatePosition = () => {
      setIsMobileViewport(window.innerWidth < 640);
      if (containerRef.current) {
        const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
        setMobilePopoverTop(Math.max(88, Math.round(rect.bottom + 6)));
      }
    };
    updatePosition();
    window.addEventListener('resize', updatePosition);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', updatePosition, true);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('resize', updatePosition);
      window.removeEventListener('scroll', updatePosition, true);
    };
  }, [isOpen, value]);

  // Đồng bộ giá trị input khi url thay đổi
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSearchPage || isQuickOrderPage) {
      setValue(params.get('q') || '');
    } else {
      setValue('');
    }
  }, [location.pathname, params, isSearchPage, isQuickOrderPage]);

  // Nạp lịch sử tìm kiếm khi mở popover
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setRecentSearches(getStoredRecentSearches());
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  // Click ngoài đóng popover
  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setIsOpen(false);
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
  }, []);

  // Debounced Live Search khi value có nội dung
  useEffect(() => {
    const query = value.trim();
    if (!query || !isOpen) {
      setLiveProducts([]);
      setIsSearching(false);
      return;
    }

    setIsSearching(true);
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      api
        .products({ search: query, page: 0 }, controller.signal)
        .then((res) => {
          const raw = res.products || [];
          const strict = searchProducts(
            raw,
            query,
            (p) => ({ text: `${p.sku || ''} ${p.name} ${t(p.name)} ${p.category || ''}`, name: t(p.name) }),
            (p) => Boolean(p.thumbUrl || p.imageUrl),
            20
          );
          setLiveProducts(strict.length > 0 ? strict : raw);
        })
        .catch((err) => {
          if (err.name !== 'AbortError') {
            setLiveProducts([]);
          }
        })
        .finally(() => {
          setIsSearching(false);
        });
    }, 180);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [value, isOpen]);

  const saveRecent = (keyword: string) => {
    const trimmed = keyword.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return;
    const current = getStoredRecentSearches();
    const updated = [trimmed, ...current.filter((item) => item.toLowerCase() !== trimmed.toLowerCase())].slice(
      0,
      MAX_RECENT_SEARCHES
    );
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
      setRecentSearches(updated);
    } catch {
      // Bỏ qua nếu storage đầy
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveRecent = (itemToRemove: string) => {
    const current = getStoredRecentSearches();
    const updated = current.filter((item) => item.toLowerCase() !== itemToRemove.toLowerCase());
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
      setRecentSearches(updated);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  };

  const handleClearAllRecent = () => {
    try {
      localStorage.removeItem(RECENT_SEARCHES_KEY);
      setRecentSearches([]);
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  };

  const handleSearchSubmit = (text: string) => {
    const q = text.trim();
    if (q) {
      saveRecent(q);
    }
    setIsOpen(false);

    if (isQuickOrderPage) {
      if (q) {
        navigate(`/dat-hang?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`, { replace: true });
      } else {
        navigate('/dat-hang', { replace: true });
      }
    } else {
      if (q) {
        navigate(`/tim-kiem?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleClear = () => {
    setValue('');
    setLiveProducts([]);
    if (isQuickOrderPage) {
      navigate('/dat-hang', { replace: true });
    } else if (isSearchPage) {
      navigate('/tim-kiem', { replace: true });
    }
  };

  const trendingCategories = (home?.categories || []).slice(0, 6);

  return (
    <div ref={containerRef} className={`relative ${className}`}>
      <form
        role="search"
        className="relative"
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          handleSearchSubmit(value);
        }}
      >
        <Search size={17} className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-[#8a9690]" />
        <input
          ref={inputRef}
          type="text"
          value={value}
          onFocus={() => setIsOpen(true)}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setValue(e.target.value);
            if (!isOpen) setIsOpen(true);
          }}
          onKeyDown={(e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') {
              setIsOpen(false);
              inputRef.current?.blur();
            }
          }}
          placeholder={t('Tìm rau củ, thịt cá...')}
          aria-label={t('Tìm sản phẩm')}
          enterKeyHint="search"
          autoComplete="off"
          className={`h-10 w-full rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/12 bg-[#f5f7f3] pl-8.5 sm:pl-9 ${value ? 'pr-21 sm:pr-24' : 'pr-14 sm:pr-16'} text-[12.5px] sm:text-[13px] text-[#17231d] placeholder:text-[#6e7a73] focus:border-[#0f7a4f] focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#0f7a4f]/15 [&::-webkit-search-cancel-button]:hidden [&::-webkit-search-decoration]:hidden`}
        />

        {value && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleClear}
            aria-label={t('Xóa tìm kiếm')}
            className="absolute right-13 sm:right-15 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-[#59665f] hover:bg-[#17231d]/8 hover:text-[#17231d] cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X size={15} />
          </button>
        )}

        <button
          type="submit"
          className="absolute right-1 top-1/2 h-8 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] px-2.5 sm:px-3.5 text-[11.5px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34] cursor-pointer"
        >
          {t('Tìm')}
        </button>
      </form>

      {/* POPOVER GỢI Ý THÔNG MINH B2B KIỂU KAMEREO */}
      {isOpen && (
        <>
          <div
            style={{ top: `${mobilePopoverTop - 4}px` }}
            className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 bg-black/30 backdrop-blur-[1px] sm:hidden"
            onMouseDown={(e) => {
              e.preventDefault();
              setIsOpen(false);
            }}
            onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
          <div
            style={
              isMobileViewport
                ? { top: `${mobilePopoverTop}px`, maxHeight: `calc(100dvh - ${mobilePopoverTop + 82}px)` }
                : undefined
            }
            className="fixed inset-x-3 z-50 flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#d7e5dc] bg-white shadow-[0_20px_50px_rgba(12,48,31,0.22)] sm:absolute sm:inset-x-0 sm:top-[calc(100%+8px)] sm:max-h-[74vh]"
          >
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain divide-y divide-[#17231d]/6">
              {/* TRẠNG THÁI A: KHI Ô TÌM KIẾM ĐANG RỖNG */}
              {!value.trim() ? (
                <div>
                  {/* Phân khu 1: Thanh Tiện ích Đặt Bếp B2B */}
                  <div className="bg-linear-to-r from-[#f8faf7] via-white to-[#f5fbf7] p-2.5 sm:p-3 border-b border-[#17231d]/6">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-1 mb-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Zap size={15} className="text-[#e0742f] fill-[#e0742f]" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Tiện ích đặt bếp nhanh')}</span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-[#0f7a4f] bg-[#0f7a4f]/10 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap">
                    {t('Chuẩn ISO 22000 · Giao sớm 05:00')}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Link
                    to="/dat-hang"
                    onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                    className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-[#17231d]/10 hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] text-[11.5px] sm:text-xs font-semibold text-[#17231d] whitespace-nowrap shadow-2xs transition group"
                  >
                    <Zap size={14} className="shrink-0 text-[#e0742f] group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform" />
                    <span>{t('Bảng đặt nhanh')}</span>
                  </Link>
                  <Link
                    to="/gio-hang?excel=1"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setIsOpen(false);
                      if (location.pathname === '/gio-hang') {
                        window.requestAnimationFrame(() => scrollToExcelOrderSection());
                      }
                    }}
                    className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-[#17231d]/10 hover:border-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] text-[11.5px] sm:text-xs font-semibold text-[#17231d] whitespace-nowrap shadow-2xs transition group"
                  >
                    <FileSpreadsheet size={14} className="shrink-0 text-[#0f7a4f] group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform" />
                    <span>{t('Đặt từ file Excel')}</span>
                  </Link>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Phân khu 2: Lịch sử tìm kiếm gần đây */}
              {recentSearches.length > 0 && (
                <div className="p-2.5 sm:p-3.5 border-b border-[#17231d]/6">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[#59665f]">
                      <Clock size={13} />
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{t('Bạn đã tìm')}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleClearAllRecent}
                      className="text-[11px] text-[#8a9690] hover:text-rose-500 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                      title={t('Xóa toàn bộ lịch sử tìm kiếm')}
                    >
                      <Trash2 size={12} />
                      <span>{t('Xóa tất cả')}</span>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                    {recentSearches.map((term) => (
                      <div
                        key={term}
                        onClick={() => {
                          setValue(term);
                          handleSearchSubmit(term);
                        }}
                        className="cursor-pointer bg-[#f5f7f3] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] hover:text-[#0f7a4f] text-[#17231d] text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#17231d]/8 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors group"
                      >
                        <span>{t(term)}</span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={(e) => {
                            e.stopPropagation();
                            handleRemoveRecent(term);
                          }}
                          className="text-[#8a9690] hover:text-rose-500 rounded p-0.5 -mr-1"
                          title={t('Xóa')}
                        >
                          <X size={12} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Phân khu 3: Tìm kiếm thịnh hành */}
              <div className="p-2.5 sm:p-3.5 border-b border-[#17231d]/6">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2">
                  <Flame size={15} className="text-[#e0742f] fill-[#e0742f]" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Tìm kiếm thịnh hành')}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {TRENDING_KEYWORDS.map((kw, idx) => (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      key={kw}
                      onClick={() => {
                        setValue(kw);
                        handleSearchSubmit(kw);
                      }}
                      className={`cursor-pointer bg-[#f8faf7] hover:bg-[#fef5ee] hover:border-[#e0742f]/30 hover:text-[#c4601f] text-[#2c3a33] text-xs font-medium px-2.5 py-1 sm:px-3 sm:py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/8 transition-all ${idx >= 8 ? 'hidden sm:inline-flex' : ''}`}
                    >
                      {t(kw)}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Phân khu 4: Nhóm hàng xu hướng cho bếp */}
              {trendingCategories.length > 0 && (
                <div className="p-2.5 sm:p-3.5 bg-[#fcfdfa]">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2.5">
                    <LayoutGrid size={15} className="text-[#0f7a4f]" />
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d]">{t('Nhóm hàng xu hướng cho bếp')}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                    {trendingCategories.map((cat) => (
                      <Link
                        to={`/danh-muc/${cat.slug}`}
                        key={cat.slug}
                        onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 rounded-xl border border-[#17231d]/8 bg-white hover:bg-[#f1f7f3] hover:border-[#0f7a4f]/30 transition group"
                      >
                        {cat.imageUrl ? (
                          <img
                            src={cat.imageUrl}
                            alt={cat.name}
                            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg object-cover shrink-0 border border-[#17231d]/6"
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f]/10 flex items-center justify-center text-[#0f7a4f] shrink-0">
                            <PackageOpen size={16} />
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f] truncate">
                          {t(cat.name)}
                        </span>
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            /* TRẠNG THÁI B: KHI Ô TÌM KIẾM CÓ TỪ KHÓA */
            <div>
              {/* Nút tìm kiếm nhanh khớp từ khóa */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSearchSubmit(value)}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 p-3 text-left hover:bg-[#f8faf7] transition-colors cursor-pointer border-b border-[#17231d]/6 text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#0f7a4f]"
              >
                <Search size={16} className="shrink-0 text-[#0f7a4f]" />
                <span className="flex-1 truncate">
                  {t('Tìm tất cả mặt hàng khớp với')} <span className="font-bold underline">"{value.trim()}"</span>
                </span>
                <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-[#8a9690] shrink-0" />
              </button>

              {/* Danh sách kết quả gợi ý trực tiếp */}
              {isSearching ? (
                <div className="p-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-[#59665f]">
                  <Loader2 size={24} className="animate-spin text-[#0f7a4f]" />
                  <span className="text-xs">{t('Đang tìm kiếm kho hàng 5.000+ sản phẩm...')}</span>
                </div>
              ) : liveProducts.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="divide-y divide-[#17231d]/6">
                  {liveProducts.slice(0, 8).map((product) => {
                    const qtyInCart = quantityOf(product.id);
                    const unitLabel = productUnit(product.unit || 'Kg');
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={product.id}
                        onClick={() => {
                          saveRecent(product.name);
                          handleSearchSubmit(product.name);
                        }}
                        className="flex items-center gap-3 p-2.5 sm:p-3 hover:bg-[#f5fbf7] transition-colors cursor-pointer group"
                      >
                        {/* Thumbnail */}
                        <ProductThumbnail product={product} size="sm" className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg shrink-0" />

                        {/* Thông tin sản phẩm */}
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <p className="text-xs sm:text-[13px] font-bold text-[#17231d] group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f] transition-colors line-clamp-1">
                            {productName(product.name)}
                          </p>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-[#59665f] mt-0.5">
                            {product.sku && (
                              <span className="font-mono text-[10px] font-bold bg-[#17231d]/6 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded shrink-0">
                                {product.sku}
                              </span>
                            )}
                            <span className="whitespace-nowrap">{unitLabel}</span>
                            {product.category && (
                              <span className="truncate">· {productName(product.category)}</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Giá sản phẩm */}
                        <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                          {product.priceOnRequest ? (
                            <span className="block text-xs font-bold text-[#e0742f]">
                              {t('Chờ sale báo giá')}
                            </span>
                          ) : (
                            <span className="block text-sm font-extrabold text-[#0f7a4f]">
                              {money(product.price)}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          <span className="block text-[10px] text-[#8a9690]">/{unitLabel}</span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Nút thêm nhanh vào giỏ */}
                        <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 pl-1">
                          {qtyInCart > 0 && (
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-[#0f7a4f]/15 text-[#0f7a4f]">
                              x{qtyInCart}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={(e) => {
                              e.stopPropagation();
                              addItem(product, 1);
                              saveRecent(product.name);
                            }}
                            className="size-8 rounded-lg bg-[#0f7a4f] text-white hover:bg-[#0b4f34] flex items-center justify-center transition-transform active:scale-95 shadow-xs cursor-pointer"
                            title={`+1 ${unitLabel} vào đơn`}
                            aria-label={`Thêm 1 ${unitLabel} ${product.name} vào đơn`}
                          >
                            <Plus size={16} strokeWidth={2.5} />
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="p-6 text-center text-xs text-[#59665f]">
                  <p className="font-semibold text-[#17231d]">
                    {t('Không tìm thấy mặt hàng phù hợp với')} "{value.trim()}"
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-[#8a9690] mt-1">
                    {t('Thử tìm bằng các từ khóa phổ biến: thịt, cá, rau củ, trứng, gia vị...')}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Dòng cuối cùng: Xem tất cả kết quả */}
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleSearchSubmit(value)}
                className="w-full p-2.5 text-center text-xs font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] transition-colors border-t border-[#17231d]/6 cursor-pointer"
              >
                {t('Xem tất cả kết quả cho')} "{value.trim()}" →
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
            </div>

            {/* Thanh đóng Popover dính đáy riêng cho Mobile */}
            <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-t border-[#17231d]/8 bg-[#f8faf7] px-3 py-2 sm:hidden">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-[#59665f]">
                {t('Chọn sản phẩm hoặc từ khóa gợi ý')}
              </span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-white px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-[#17231d] ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/12 active:scale-95 cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={13} />
                {t('Đóng')}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function CategoryThumbnail({ category }: { category: StoreCategory }) {
  const [error, setError] = useState(false);
  return (
    <span className="flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[#eaf6ef] ring-1 ring-[#0f7a4f]/10">
      {category.imageUrl && !error ? (
        <img
          src={category.imageUrl}
          alt=""
          loading="lazy"
          onError={() => setError(true)}
          className="h-full w-full object-cover"
        />
      ) : (
        <Package size={16} className="text-[#0f7a4f]/60" />
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

function CategorySidebar({ categories }: { categories: StoreCategory[] }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const [hoveredSlug, setHoveredSlug] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const hoveredCategory = categories.find((c) => c.slug === hoveredSlug) || null;
  const subCategories = hoveredCategory?.productCategories || [];
  const showFlyout = Boolean(hoveredCategory && subCategories.length > 1);

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label={t('Danh mục hàng hóa')}
      onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredSlug(null)}
      className="relative hidden min-h-85 rounded-2xl bg-white ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/6 lg:block"
    >
      <div className="no-scrollbar absolute inset-0 flex flex-col overflow-y-auto p-1.5">
        {categories.map((c) => {
          const isHovered = hoveredSlug === c.slug;
          const hasSub = Boolean(c.productCategories && c.productCategories.length > 1);
          return (
            <Link
              key={c.slug}
              to={`/danh-muc/${c.slug}`}
              onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredSlug(hasSub ? c.slug : null)}
              className={`group flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl px-2 py-1 text-[13.5px] transition ${
                isHovered
                  ? 'bg-[#eaf6ef] font-bold text-[#0f7a4f]'
                  : 'font-semibold text-[#17231d] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] hover:text-[#0b4f34]'
              }`}
            >
              <CategoryThumbnail category={c} />
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{t(c.name)}</span>
              {hasSub && (
                <ChevronRight
                  size={16}
                  className={`shrink-0 transition ${
                    isHovered
                      ? 'translate-x-0.5 text-[#0f7a4f]'
                      : 'text-[#8a9690] group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:text-[#0f7a4f]'
                  }`}
                />
              )}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
        <Link
          to="/danh-muc"
          onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredSlug(null)}
          className="mt-auto flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl px-2 py-1 text-[13px] font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:bg-[#eaf6ef]"
        >
          <span className="flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[#0f7a4f]/10">
            <LayoutGrid size={15} />
          </span>
          {t('Xem tất cả danh mục')}
        </Link>
      </div>

      {/* Bảng Flyout mục nhỏ thực tế từ API bên phải khi lia chuột vào danh mục */}
      {showFlyout && hoveredCategory && (
        <div className="absolute top-0 left-full bottom-0 z-30 hidden pl-2 lg:flex">
          <div className="flex h-full w-68 xl:w-76 flex-col justify-between overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl bg-white p-4 shadow-[0_20px_50px_rgba(12,48,31,0.16)] ring-1 ring-[#17231d]/8">
            <div className="space-y-1">
              {subCategories.map((subName) => (
                <Link
                  key={subName}
                  to={`/danh-muc/${hoveredCategory.slug}?nhom=${encodeURIComponent(subName)}`}
                  onClick={() => setHoveredSlug(null)}
                  className="group/sub flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-[14px] font-semibold text-[#17231d] transition hover:bg-[#eaf6ef] hover:text-[#0f7a4f]"
                >
                  <span className="truncate">{t(subName)}</span>
                  <ChevronRight
                    size={15}
                    className="shrink-0 text-[#8a9690] opacity-0 transition group-hover/sub:translate-x-0.5 group-hover/sub:text-[#0f7a4f] group-hover/sub:opacity-100"
                  />
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
            <Link
              to={`/danh-muc/${hoveredCategory.slug}`}
              onClick={() => setHoveredSlug(null)}
              className="mt-3 inline-flex items-center justify-end gap-1 border-t border-[#17231d]/8 pt-3 px-2 text-xs font-bold text-[#0f7a4f] hover:text-[#0b4f34] hover:underline"
            >
              <span>
                {t('Xem tất cả')} {t(hoveredCategory.name)}
              </span>
              <ChevronRight size={14} />
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </nav>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowUpDown, Check, ChevronDown, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, PackageSearch } from 'lucide-react';
import { api, ApiError, type Product } from '../lib/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useLanguage } from '../contexts/LanguageContext';

import StoreProductGrid, { StoreGridSkeleton, useGridColumns } from '../components/store/StoreProductGrid';
import CategoryRail from '../components/store/CategoryRail';

type Sort = 'default' | 'price_asc' | 'price_desc' | 'name';

const SORT_OPTIONS: { value: Sort; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'default', label: 'Phổ biến' },
  { value: 'price_asc', label: 'Giá thấp đến cao' },
  { value: 'price_desc', label: 'Giá cao đến thấp' },
  { value: 'name', label: 'Tên A–Z' },
];

function SortDropdown({
  value,
  onChange,
  align = 'left',
}: {
  value: Sort;
  onChange: (next: Sort) => void;
  align?: 'left' | 'right';
}) {
  const { t } = useLanguage();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const handlePointerDown = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (rootRef.current && !rootRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        setOpen(false);
      }
    };
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false);
    };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handlePointerDown);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handlePointerDown);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [open]);

  const currentLabel = SORT_OPTIONS.find((opt) => opt.value === value)?.label ?? 'Phổ biến';

  return (
    <div ref={rootRef} className="relative flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
      <span className="hidden sm:inline text-xs sm:text-sm font-semibold text-[#17231d]">
        {t('Sắp xếp theo')}:
      </span>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((prev) => !prev)}
        aria-haspopup="listbox"
        aria-expanded={open}
        className={`flex h-9 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border bg-white px-3.5 text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-[#17231d] transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-2xs ${
          open
            ? 'border-[#0f7a4f] ring-2 ring-[#0f7a4f]/15'
            : 'border-[#17231d]/15 hover:border-[#0f7a4f]/50 hover:bg-[#f8faf7]'
        }`}
      >
        <ArrowUpDown size={14} className="text-[#0f7a4f] shrink-0" />
        <span className="whitespace-nowrap">{t(currentLabel)}</span>
        <ChevronDown
          size={15}
          className={`text-[#59665f] transition-transform duration-200 ${open ? 'rotate-180 text-[#0f7a4f]' : ''}`}
        />
      </button>

      {open && (
        <div
          role="listbox"
          className={`absolute ${align === 'right' ? 'right-0' : 'left-0'} top-[calc(100%+6px)] z-30 min-w-50 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-[#17231d]/10 bg-white p-1.5 shadow-xl`}
        >
          {SORT_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
            const active = opt.value === value;
            return (
              <button
                key={opt.value}
                type="button"
                role="option"
                aria-selected={active}
                onClick={() => {
                  onChange(opt.value);
                  setOpen(false);
                }}
                className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs sm:text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer ${
                  active
                    ? 'bg-[#eaf6ef] font-bold text-[#0b4f34]'
                    : 'font-medium text-[#17231d] hover:bg-[#f4f8f5]'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="whitespace-nowrap">{t(opt.label)}</span>
                {active && <Check size={14} className="text-[#0f7a4f] shrink-0" />}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

  if (sort === 'default') return products;

function getPageNumbers(current: number, total: number): (number | string)[] {
  if (total <= 7) return Array.from({ length: total }, (_, i) => i + 1);
  if (current <= 4) return [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, '...', total];
  if (current >= total - 3) return [1, '...', total - 4, total - 3, total - 2, total - 1, total];
  return [1, '...', current - 1, current, current + 1, '...', total];
}

[FILE MỚI] Khung Đặt hàng nhanh bằng file Excel tích hợp trực tiếp trong trang Giỏ hàng; sửa 2 nút "Tải file mẫu" và "Chọn file Excel tải lên" dùng flex không bị cắt chữ "..."; hàm scrollToExcelOrderSection() luôn cuộn trúng đích.
